Conclusion: VSI不是通用最优的拒答信号，但它作为条件性集成组件，对于发现哪些样本模型“无视”视觉输入极具价值。该研究提醒当前VLM评测需修正默认假设，未来需关注模型对视觉信号的真实利用。

Abstract: Vision-language models are evaluated by aggregate accuracy on multimodal benchmarks, a practice that implicitly assumes the model uses its visual input. We show this assumption fails on 40%--97% of samples across six VLMs and three perceptual benchmarks: blurring the question-relevant visual region leaves the next-token distribution nearly unchanged. We name this phenomenon the Visual Insensitivity Gap and quantify it with a per-sample Visual Sensitivity Index (VSI). The gap is a property of samples, not of models: VSI ranks correlate across models (grand-mean Spearman rho=+0.40, permutation p<10^-3), so the same samples are flagged insensitive by VLMs sharing no architectural detail beyond a contrastively pretrained vision tower. The mechanism is concrete: on the insensitive samples, a linear probe on each model's own vision tower distinguishes perturbed from clean images at 0.72--0.79 accuracy, yet the model's argmax token changes on only 2%--11% of the same samples, an encoder--LLM gap above 0.65 on every model. Mapping VSI's diagnostic utility cell by cell surfaces a strong regime (multi-choice reasoning on capable VLMs: AUROC=0.85--0.87) and a weak regime (well-calibrated factuality, where softmax confidence already leads). VSI is not a universal best abstention signal; it is a sample-intrinsic indicator of vision-ignoring failure, best used as a conditional ensemble component.

</details>


### [56] [Vision-Language-Guided Pseudo-Labels for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation in Semantic Segmentation for Waste Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00898)
*Udo Schlegel,Shubhangi,Gabriel Dax,Sai Rahul Kaminwar,Florian Karl,Thomas Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种跨模态伪标签管道，在无需目标域标注的情况下，实现无监督域适应的语义分割。应用于自动驾驶和工业垃圾分拣等实际场景并取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用（如自动驾驶、工业垃圾分拣）中，语义分割的数据标注成本高且难以大规模获取，因此需要一种无需目标域标注的域适应方法，提升模型泛化能力。

Method: 利用SAM生成无类别区域提议，EVA-CLIP通过区域-文本相似度赋予语义标签，并通过置信度筛选可靠伪标签用于自训练分割模型。可选的BLIP对歧义区域进行语言引导的验证，以提升伪标签质量。整体实现跨模态自监督域适应。

Result: 在合成到真实自动驾驶、实验室到工厂工业垃圾分拣两个域转移任务中，该管道在无目标域标注情况下，分割表现均优于仅用源域训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 伪标签的质量比数量更关键，跨模态语言引导的方法能有效提升自监督域适应语义分割的自动化水平，尤其适用于关键部署场景。

Abstract: Obtaining labeled data for semantic segmentation in applied settings (e.g., autonomous driving, industrial waste sorting) is expensive and often infeasible at scale. We present a cross-modal pseudo-labeling pipeline that enables unsupervised domain adaptation without any target-domain annotations. The pipeline is built on two core foundation models: SAM generates class-agnostic region proposals, and EVA-CLIP assigns semantic labels based on region-text similarity, with confidence filtering ensuring that only reliable pseudo-labels are used for self-training a segmentation model. As an optional extension, BLIP provides language-grounded verification for ambiguous regions, thereby improving pseudo-label quality without altering the overall pipeline. Evaluated on two domain shifts, synthetic-to-real autonomous driving and, with a primary focus, lab-to-factory industrial waste sorting, the pipeline consistently improves over source-only baselines. Our results demonstrate that pseudo-label quality, not quantity, is a decisive factor in self-training under domain shift, and that cross-modal language grounding offers a practical path to reliable automatic annotation in deployment-critical applications.

</details>


### [57] [HELIOS: From midnight to noon, continuous outdoor urban scene relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00901)
*Hala Djeghim,Nathan Piasco,Luis Roldão,Moussab Bennehar,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Céline Loscos,Désiré Sidibé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的图像重光照方法HELIOS，可在无标注真实数据集上对驾驶图像的白天和夜晚光照进行自然、细致的转换，效果优于当前最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶场景数据集大多采集于特定时间，缺乏多样光照条件；多数方法依赖合成或成对监督，严重限制泛化能力和实用性。

Method: HELIOS不依赖配对数据，基于未标注真实数据，采用基于反照率(albedo)的条件扩散模型，通过循环一致性防止图像身份丢失，并针对夜晚的低可见性设计结构稳定性迁移；此外，利用GPS太阳角替代文字提示，实现连续细腻的光照调节。

Result: 通过广泛实验和用户研究，HELIOS在白天-夜晚双向任务中生成结构一致、极为真实的效果，全面超越现有方法。

Conclusion: HELIOS实现在现实世界复杂光照条件下的高质量图像重光照，为无人驾驶等领域数据增强和模型适应性带来新突破。

Abstract: Modifying the illumination of driving images is a fundamental challenge, as most datasets are captured at specific times of day. Existing methods rely on synthetic data or paired multi-illumination supervision, which limits their generalization to the diverse and challenging conditions of real-world scenarios. To address this, we propose HELIOS, a novel image relighting approach that relies on unlabeled real-world datasets without requiring any paired images for training. Our approach integrates albedo-based conditioning into a cycle-consistent diffusion pipeline to prevent identity collapse and ensure accurate domain translation. To handle low-visibility nighttime conditions, we introduce a robust albedo distillation strategy that transfers structural stability from the daytime domain. Additionally, we replace traditional text prompts with a fine-grained control mechanism based on GPS-derived solar angles, enabling smooth and continuous lighting manipulation across the day-night cycle. Through extensive evaluation and a user study, we demonstrate that HELIOS produces structurally consistent and realistic results in both night-to-day and day-to-night tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [58] [A multicenter benchmark and clinically structured metric for coronary CTA report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00909)
*Zhiyu Ye,Yue Sun,Limiao Zou,Cheng Xu,Keting Xu,Tong Hu,Yue Yu,Hairong Zheng,Yining Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于多中心的冠状动脉CT血管造影（CCTA）报告生成评估基准和一种新的临床结构化评估指标CSM_CCTA，并用其系统评价了多个开源医学图文模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化CCTA报告生成的评估缺乏统一的多中心基准和能够反映临床结构的信息评价指标，导致模型评估结果与实际临床需求脱节。

Method: 作者构建了包含4个中心、3021例CCTA影像及818份患者报告配对的多中心基准，开发了CSM_CCTA指标，按临床指南定义患者、血管、节段等不同层级变量，在解剖学最细粒度层面按专家赋权对报告进行对比，并用专家打分的数据对权重进行估算和相关性验证。

Result: CSM_CCTA与放射科医生评分相关性极高（r=0.97，显著优于其他指标），且对等效表述具鲁棒性，对信息缺失敏感。C2RG模型在该基准下表现最佳，但仍不理想。泛用模型生成的无关报告比高达98.7%。

Conclusion: 该基准和CSM_CCTA指标为CCTA报告生成模型的标准化对比和临床结构化评估提供了有效工具，有助于推动相关模型向更贴合临床的方向发展。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of automated coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA) report generation requires standardized multicentre benchmarks and clinically structured metrics. We established a four-centre benchmark comprising 3,021 CCTA series from 818 patient-report pairs to evaluate seven open-source three-dimensional vision-language models. We developed CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$, a clinically structured metric for CCTA report evaluation, with patient-, vessel-, and segment-level variables defined according to clinical guidelines. Report pairs are compared at the finest shared anatomical level, and the contributions of different clinical components are weighted based on expert assessments. We estimated these weights using 70 expert-scored cases and evaluated clinical alignment in a non-overlapping set of 30 cases. CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ showed a strong correlation with radiologist scores (Pearson's $r=0.97$, $p<0.001$), exceeding the next-best metric, FORTE ($r=0.70$), by 0.27, and agreed with expert preferences in 115 of 160 pairwise comparisons (71.9\%). Under controlled perturbations, CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ remained stable to clinically equivalent wording and decreased monotonically with progressive information omission. In the multicenter benchmark, the CCTA-trained C2RG model achieved the highest CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ scores across all four hospitals, although its performance remained far from optimal. In contrast, CCTA-irrelevant reports accounted for up to 98.7\% of the outputs from generalist models. Together, the benchmark provides a standardized setting for model comparison, while CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ enables clinically structured evaluation of finding agreement and anatomical specificity. These results support a more clinically aligned and anatomically resolved approach to evaluating CCTA report generation. Code is available at https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenMedIA/CSM_CCTA.

</details>


### [59] [On-the-Fly3R: Towards Robust Online 3D Reconstruction with Feed-Forward 3R Models for Large-Scale UAV Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00923)
*Zhe Shen,Liyuan Lou,Yifei Yu,Guanbo Wang,Quanjian Ji,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-the-Fly3R的训练-free、在线递进式3D重建方法，专为无人机(UAV)大规模影像设计，可支持无序输入、高效扩展及一致性保障，并显著优于现有主流流式3D重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流3D重建方法由于Transformer注意力机制消耗大量内存，难以应对无人机大规模场景；现有的可扩展流式3D重建假设输入具时空连续性，无法应用于弱序或无序的跨带无人机采集影像。因此，亟需适应无序输入、高效并一致的3D建模方法。

Method: 提出On-the-Fly3R框架，特点包括(1) 无需训练、可在线逐步完善3D重建；(2) 基于检索动态构建空间相关图像子集，实现对无序输入的有效重建；(3) 设计了一套‘验证-拒绝-重试’机制，提前检测融合一致性，错位图像自动剔除并尝试替代；(4) 借鉴VSLAM的检索回环构建位姿图优化，进一步缓解相机漂移问题。

Result: 在多个UAV数据集基准上评测，On-the-Fly3R可扩展支持超过5,000张图片、平方公里级场景，在精度上大幅优于多种SOTA流式3D重建方法。

Conclusion: On-the-Fly3R显著提升了大规模UAV场景下3D重建的可扩展性和精度，对主流3R骨干网络具有良好的升级兼容性，并且对实际无人机弱序列场景极具应用价值。

Abstract: While feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) offers efficient end-to-end modeling, its application in large-scale UAV mapping is hindered by the prohibitive memory cost of Transformer attention. Current scalable streaming 3R methods assume temporally and spatially continuous inputs, rendering them ineffective for the weakly ordered or unordered image streams common in cross-strip UAV operations. To address this, we propose On-the-Fly3R, a training-free, progressive online 3D reconstruction framework for large-scale UAV images that upgrades various 3R backbones for large-scale UAV scenarios. Our method enables reconstruction from unordered inputs via retrieval-guided dynamic subset construction, which adaptively selects spatially relevant images. To further improve the robustness, a validation-rejection-retry mechanism is designed to guarantee global consistency, performing a pre-integration consistency check and automatically rejecting misaligned images and retrying with alternative subset. Finally, inspired by VSLAM, pose graph optimization based on the retrieval loop closure is employed to mitigate camera drift. Evaluations on several UAV benchmarks show that our On-the-Fly3R successfully scales various 3R models to over 5,000 images across square-kilometer UAV scenes, delivering substantially superior accuracy compared to several SOTA streaming 3R methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sh1nZzz/On_the_Fly3R

</details>


### [60] [Beyond the Image Plane: World-Grounded Queries for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00924)
*Orcun Cetintas,Guillem Brasó,Tim Meinhardt,Laura Leal-Taixé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PLANET，一种突破图像平面的端到端多目标跟踪方法，通过提升2D数据到3D，并结合3D场景几何改进特征和时序记忆，显著提升了多目标跟踪的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目视频多目标跟踪方法仅依赖于图像平面中的外观和几何信息，导致深度与空间关系模糊，限制了跟踪精度。为克服二维观测的固有限制，亟需开发基于三维空间信息的多目标跟踪方法。

Method: 1）将现有2D跟踪数据集提升为3D。2）在查询形成时，将重建的3D场景几何特征嵌入特征和位置编码中，得到基于真实世界的查询。3）引入辅助3D位置预测任务，强化训练阶段的空间位置编码能力。4）设计双分辨率时序记忆机制，以跨越更长的时间间隔保持跟踪证据。

Result: PLANET在三个多样化的基准测试中均取得了最新最优的多目标跟踪表现，有效提升了跟踪精度与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合3D场景理解和先进的时序建模，PLANET有效克服了仅依赖2D信息的不足，为多目标跟踪任务提供了更强的空间与时序推断能力。

Abstract: Monocular videos record 3D scenes as sequences of 2D image-plane projections, obscuring depth and spatial relationships. Multi-object trackers localize and associate objects primarily using appearance and geometry observed only in the image plane, inheriting these ambiguities. To address this limitation, we introduce PLANET, an end-to-end multi-object tracker designed to move beyond the image plane. As an enabling step, we lift existing 2D tracking datasets into 3D. We then form world-grounded queries by embedding reconstructed 3D scene geometry into the features and positional encodings used during query formation. An auxiliary 3D location prediction task further encourages the queries to encode object positions during training. A complementary dual-resolution temporal memory preserves this evidence across longer temporal gaps. As a result, PLANET achieves state-of-the-art performance across three diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [From Terminology to Diagrams: Visual-Instruction Generation for Scientific Diagram Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00948)
*Raul Ortega,José Manuel Gómez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动生成大规模科学图示指令数据的框架，并据此构建了SciGram数据集，极大提升了VLM在科学图表领域的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在实景图像的视觉问答任务中表现优异，但在以功能和关系为核心的科学图示理解任务中仍存在明显不足。为解决此问题，亟需大规模、针对科学图示的高质量多模态数据集作为支撑。

Method: 作者设计了一套自动化流程：首先从科学课程中提取学科术语和概念，合成原子级事实；随后从网络检索相关科学图示，并针对每张图生成图注和多项选择问题，进而构建了SciGram数据集。

Result: 基于该流程，作者获得了包含19.4万张图表和140万条指令的SciGram数据集。通过在此数据集上微调模型，在多个科学图示问答基准（如TQA、ScienceQA、AI2D）上，大幅超越或匹配了当前最先进的VLM模型，且所需训练数据量更少。将SciGram数据用于LLaVA OneVision等模型进一步刷新了领域内SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 基于术语的指令生成策略为提升VLM在科学领域的推理能力提供了有效方式。作者已公开SciGram数据集及相关模型，方便后续科学图示理解研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in visual question answering with natural images. However, they continue to struggle with scientific diagrams, which are designed to convey functional or relational meaning rather than literal scenes. We therefore introduce a framework for generating large-scale diagram-grounded instruction data by leveraging terminology derived from scientific curricula. Our approach systematically extracts domain concepts, synthesizes atomic facts, retrieves relevant diagrams from the web, and generates multimodal supervision in the form of diagram captions and multiple-choice questions. Using this pipeline, we construct SciGram, a dataset of over 194K diagrams and 1.4M visual instructions across life, earth, and physical sciences. Despite relying on noisy web data and synthetic annotations, models fine-tuned on SciGram achieve substantial improvements on diagram-centric benchmarks, including TQA, ScienceQA, and AI2D, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art VLMs while using fewer training instances. Furthermore, augmenting existing models such as LLaVA OneVision with SciGram establishes new state-of-the-art performance on diagram question answering. Our results highlight the effectiveness of terminology-grounded instruction generation as a general strategy for improving vision-language reasoning in scientific domains. To support future research in scientific diagram understanding, we release both the SciGram dataset and models.

</details>


### [62] [CERF: Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00951)
*Jiuwu Hao,Ziyi Ni,Liguo Sun,Yuting Wan,Yueyang Wu,Ti Xiang,Haolin Song,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通信高效且无需重新训练的新型多智能体协同感知框架CERF，通过引入虚拟模态（Poture）和历史感知推断，实现显著降低通信开销且保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前协同感知方法多依赖密集特征图传输，导致通信负担大且异构性挑战限制其实际部署。因此，有必要开发一种通信高效、适应开放异构场景的协同感知新框架。

Method: 提出CERF框架，通过将其他智能体感知结果生成虚拟模态（Poture），增强本地BEV特征。同时结合卡尔曼滤波跟踪和运动预测，对历史感知结果进行当前预测，减少通信延迟。

Result: 实验表明，CERF在各种下游任务上实现了与主流协同方法相当的性能，同时通信开销降低达95%。此外，CERF支持异构未知新智能体的无缝接入，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: CERF在大幅提升通信效率的同时，确保了协同感知性能，有望大大推动多智能体协同感知实际落地应用。

Abstract: Collaborative perception shares information among multiple agents to obtain a comprehensive scene representation, enhancing the perceptual capability of individual agents. However, most existing methods rely on transmitting and fusing dense feature maps for collaboration, which incurs inevitable communication overhead and heterogeneity challenges, limiting their practicality for real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we propose CERF, a novel Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free framework for open heterogeneous collaborative perception. In CERF, we introduce a new virtual modality (termed Poture), which is generated from the perception outputs of other agents, to augment the extracted Bird's Eye View (BEV) features of the ego agent. To mitigate transmission delays, we employ a Kalman-filter based tracker and a motion forecasting model to derive the current predictions from historical perception results. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CERF achieves performance comparable to mainstream intermediate-collaboration methods while reducing communication overhead by 95% across various downstream tasks. Furthermore, CERF enables seamless integration of unknown heterogeneous agents into the existing collaborative framework without additional retraining costs. Code is available at https://github.com/uestchjw/CERF.

</details>


### [63] [ASSERT: Adaptive Stochastic Sampling for Robust Diffusion Models on Analog Compute-in-Memory Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00955)
*Yuannuo Feng,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Yuxin Xie,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了模拟器件中的存算一体(CIM)硬件噪声对扩散模型推理的影响，并提出了ASSERT——一种无需额外训练、能提升扩散模型在CIM设备上鲁棒性的采样器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型近年在图像生成任务上表现优秀，但计算过程中需多次迭代去噪，计算量大。CIM可以加速推理，但其固有的空间存储误差和噪声累积可能影响最终生成质量。扩散模型对硬件噪声的时间敏感性及其影响，目前研究不多。

Method: 作者通过噪声建模，并结合多片真实CIM芯片的实验测量数据，校准并验证了硬件噪声模型。分析了扩散模型生成过程中不同阶段对硬件噪声的脆弱性，并利用一阶轨迹分析解释原因。根据发现，提出ASSERT采样器，在初始阶段注入较高随机性，随后平滑过渡到确定性去噪，无需修改模型参数和增加网络前向次数。

Result: ASSERT方法在多个实验设置下均取得了明显性能提升：在高分辨率数据集上FID指标降低最高可达2.58倍，在CIFAR-10步数实验中低至7.68倍。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性揭示扩散模型早期去噪阶段更易受CIM硬件噪声影响，并提出无需重新训练的ASSERT采样器，可大幅提升在带噪CIM硬件上的扩散模型生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong image generation quality but incur high iterative denoising costs. Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) can accelerate matrix-vector multiplications, yet spatial memory variations perturb weights and accumulate during sampling. Unlike conventional neural networks, diffusion models' temporal sensitivity to hardware noise remains underexplored. We investigate diffusion inference using a noise model calibrated and validated against measurements collected from multiple physical CIM chips. Our results show that the early, high-noise denoising stage is substantially more vulnerable than the final refinement stage. A first-order trajectory analysis attributes this behavior to the repeated propagation of correlated prediction errors induced by a fixed hardware mapping. Based on this observation, we propose ASSERT, a training-free sampler that uses higher stochasticity early and smoothly transitions to deterministic denoising. The injected stochasticity changes subsequent activation trajectories and thereby reduces their alignment with persistent spatial errors. Across the evaluated settings, ASSERT achieves up to 2.58$\times$ lower FID than deterministic DDIM on high-resolution datasets and 7.68$\times$ lower FID in the CIFAR-10 step-count study, without changing model parameters or the number of network evaluations.

</details>


### [64] [PredErase: Training-Free Object-and-Effect Removal with Predictive Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00956)
*Waikit Xiu,Qiang Lu,Junbiao Chen,Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PredErase，一种在冻结的生成模型（如FLUX.2和I-JEPA）上，无需训练即可更自然地移除对象及其相关阴影与接触痕迹的方法。该方法避免传统掩码填充导致的“残影”，在多个移除数据集上优于原生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象移除方法只关注掩码区域内像素填充，无法去除对象留下的阴影和接触区域，导致移除后图像仍存在“光照脚印”。而无需训练的实例移除方法缺少对被遮挡场景的合理预测，效果受限。

Method: PredErase方法引入了“接触带扩展”（M_flux），即在原掩码M_obj基础上扩展，暴露出支持面的残留区域。利用预训练的I-JEPA模型在表征空间对遮挡区域进行上下文预测，通过稀疏投影梯度对掩码内区域进行引导填充，提升填充合理性，保持掩码外区域不变。整个流程纯推理、无需训练。

Result: 在RemovalBench、RORD-Val和DEFACTO-Val等数据集上，PredErase在仅有实例掩码的前提下，较FLUX.2原生backbone取得了更好效果，但在全图外观度量方面仍落后于有监督训练的移除模型。

Conclusion: PredErase为冻结生成模型带来了无需配对数据和额外训练的联合对象与光照效应编辑能力。虽然全面替代有监督方法仍有差距，但其在无训练条件下已能有效改善对象消除后的自然度。

Abstract: Removing an object is not the same as filling its mask. Cast shadows and contact shading usually lie outside the user-provided instance mask M_obj, so a frozen Fill model that edits only that mask leaves the object's photometric footprint on nearby surfaces. Supervised removers learn this joint erasure from paired clean plates. Training-free editors freeze pretrained weights, yet most still treat M_obj as the entire editable support and steer sampling with CLIP or DINO energies that do not predict the occluded scene. We present PredErase, a training-free inference procedure on frozen FLUX.2 and I-JEPA. The method separates where Fill may rewrite pixels from what structure should occupy the hole. A contact-band expansion M_flux of M_obj exposes local residuals on the supporting plane. I-JEPA, pretrained for masked token prediction, supplies a context-conditioned hole target in representation space; sparse projected gradients align decoded Fill completions with that target inside the instance, while coordinates outside the packed support stay locked. Under instance-only masks on RemovalBench, RORD-Val, and DEFACTO-Val, PredErase improves the native FLUX.2 backbone. Supervised removers remain stronger on several full-image appearance metrics; the supported claim is training-free object-and-effect editing of frozen Fill, not replacement of paired-data erasers.

</details>


### [65] [Candidate-Expanding Routing with Permutation-Stabilized Experts for Mixed-Format Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00959)
*Hai-Dang Nguyen,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对混合格式医学视觉问答（VQA）任务的新方法，实现了更稳定的选项选择和更可靠的机器可读自由文本输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA任务既包含多选题也包含自由文本答案，现有系统在多选题的选项符号变化和自由文本答案格式错误时容易出错，导致自动评估和实际应用遇到瓶颈。该工作旨在提高两种任务的鲁棒性和可用性。

Method: 本文提出结合答案文本记忆、排序稳定的视觉-语言专家模型和稀疏候选扩展路由器，并在路由时将专家top-2答案作为 routable candidate 和记忆、top-1 一起考虑。采用循环训练调度方法，并在开放式输出中使用严格生成和确定性保护机制，保障输出格式。

Result: 在1403例回顾性内部数据集上，候选扩展使二值路由准确率从88.95%提升到91.73%，共纠正了56处错误但新增17处回退。oracle覆盖率从90.31%升至96.15%，最终配置可达92.23%。对于自由文本问题，严格机制确保100%（475/475）输出无格式错误。消融实验显示文本依赖显著，候选扩展是路由性能提升的主要因素。

Conclusion: 提出的方法显著提升了混合格式医学VQA的稳定性和输出有效性，特别是在路由和格式控制方面表现优异，但在临床正确性或实际部署中仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Mixed-format medical visual question answering (VQA) requires stable option selection and machine-readable free-text output. The two formats fail differently: multiple-choice predictions can change with option symbols or positions, while clinically plausible open answers can fail automated evaluation when serialization is malformed. We address both challenges with an answer-text memory, a permutation-stabilized vision--language expert, and a sparse candidate- expanding router. The cyclic schedule follows prior work; our contribution is to make expert top-2 a routable candidate alongside memory and expert top-1. On a 1,403-case retrospective internal analysis, this expansion improves a matched binary router from 88.95% to 91.73% (+2.78 percentage points; 95% CI 1.57--3.99), with 56 rescued errors and 17 regressions. Oracle coverage rises from 90.31% to 96.15%, and the final submitted configuration reaches 92.23% on the same retrospective split. For open questions, strict generation and deterministic guards produce 475/475 schema- valid participant-facing outputs without repair, retry, or hard-gate failure. Visual ablations reveal substantial textual dependence. Candidate expansion supplies the principal controlled routing gain; open-path evidence establishes output-contract validity rather than clinical correctness in medical use or deployment.

</details>


### [66] [Conditional Flow Matching for Cross-Field MRI Harmonisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00960)
*Baris Imre,Aram Salehi,Levente Baljer,Andrew Webb,Marius Staring,Efe Ilicak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种单模型方法，实现三种对比度与五种磁共振成像（MRI）场强间的任意方向图像转换，并在MRIxFields2026挑战赛中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁共振图像（MRI）在不同场强下成像效果差异较大，这导致不同医疗中心之间的数据难以比较和整合，需要一种能够统一不同场强和对比度MRI图像的模型。

Method: 作者将跨场强MRI图像转换问题建模为条件流匹配路径，直接学习源图像到目标图像的速度场。模型训练分三阶段：首先通过丰富的无配对数据进行降质-恢复预训练以获得恢复先验，然后在有限配对数据上进行跨场强微调，最后用对抗精修提升输出图像清晰度。推理时用二阶Heun方法整合速度场实现快速推断。

Result: 仅由无配对数据学习的恢复先验模型平均SSIM已达0.837，每进一步训练后性能提升，最终单一630万参数模型涵盖60种场强-对比度组合，平均五步即可推断单切片图像。在挑战赛评测集上SSIM达0.909，优于基于同一网络的回归和扩散基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一模型能高效、准确地实现不同场强与对比度间MRI图像转换，为多中心、多场强MRI研究和应用提供了重要工具，技术具有广泛推广前景。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance images of the same subject look markedly different across field strengths, which complicates the comparison and pooling of data across sites. We address cross-field brain-MRI translation for the MRIxFields2026 challenge, and in particular its Task~3: a single model that translates between any directed pair of the five field strengths and across three contrasts. We phrase the problem as a conditional flow matching path: because the source and target volumes are spatially registered, we learn a velocity field that carries the source slice directly to the target slice, rather than starting from noise. To learn this mapping from only three paired subjects, the unified model is trained in three stages: a degradation-bridge pretraining that distills a restoration prior from the abundant unpaired retrospective cohort, a cross-field finetuning over all directed pairs on the paired cohort, and an adversarial refinement that sharpens the output. At inference, we integrate the learned velocity with a second-order Heun solver in a handful of steps. A restoration prior learned without any paired data already reaches a mean SSIM of 0.837, and each subsequent training stage improves on it. A single 6.3M-parameter model thereby covers all 60 field-pair and contrast combinations, with inference in five solver steps per slice. On the challenge evaluation set the model reaches a mean SSIM of 0.909, averaged over the three contrasts, outperforming regression and diffusion baselines built on the identical network on all three challenge metrics.

</details>


### [67] [ReFlowSET: Representation-Aligned Latent Flow Matching for SAR-to-EO Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00968)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Seungchul Lee,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对SAR转EO（合成孔径雷达转电光影像）任务的全新框架ReFlowSET，通过联合SAR与EO的重建审核，自动选择最优的潜在编码器，大幅提升了成果的感知和分布一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAR-to-EO影像翻译方法通常沿用自然图像预训练的潜在编码器（codec），但这些编码器未必适合SAR与EO影像，导致转换质量不稳定。为了解决由codec选择带来的重建不一致问题，作者试图通过专门设计能更好适应这一任务的codec选择方法，提升整体表现。

Method: 提出ReFlowSET方法：在SAR-EO双模态重建精准性的基础上，联合审核以选择最佳潜在编码器，然后在所选编码空间中从头训练小型条件DiT（Diffusion Transformer）；采用SAR双流条件输入，并引入训练阶段的语义对齐约束，将中间噪声EO特征与由冻结视觉基础模型提取的干净EO特征对齐。注意，该对齐过程仅在训练时使用，不增加推理时开销。

Result: 在QXS-SAROPT和SAR2Opt数据集上，ReFlowSET在多个感知保真度和分布性指标上均超越了现有SOTA方法，展现出更强的跨模态转换能力。

Conclusion: ReFlowSET通过任务自适应的codec选择和创新性的训练语义约束，显著提升了SAR-to-EO转换的质量和稳定性，为类似的跨模态影像任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: SAR-to-EO image translation aims to generate electro-optical (EO) imagery from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations. Existing latent diffusion approaches typically inherit a predetermined autoencoder, although reconstruction fidelity can vary substantially across codecs and modalities. Because the latent codec affects the round-trip preservation of both SAR conditions and EO targets, codec selection constitutes a fundamental design choice; nevertheless, existing methods largely rely on codecs pretrained on natural images. To remedy this, we introduce ReFlowSET, a conditional latent flow-matching framework that selects its codec through a joint SAR--EO reconstruction audit. Rather than inheriting a heavyweight pretrained generator, ReFlowSET trains a substantially smaller conditional DiT from scratch in the selected latent space, using dual-stream SAR conditioning followed by joint feature refinement. To provide semantic guidance for this from-scratch training, intermediate noisy-EO features are aligned with clean target-EO representations extracted by a frozen vision foundation model. This alignment is used only during training and introduces no additional inference cost. Experiments on QXS-SAROPT and SAR2Opt demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse perceptual fidelity and distributional metrics. Code and pretrained weights are publicly available at https://github.com/KAIST-VICLab/ReFlowSET.

</details>


### [68] [Semi-Supervised Virtual Staining via Morphology Preservation and Histopathological Realism Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00984)
*Baoshun Wang,Weiping Lin,Linwu Wang,Yihuang Hu,Baptiste Magnier,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稳定的半监督虚拟染色框架，结合少量配对数据和大量未配对数据，提高了虚拟染色图像的质量和稳定性，并在多个翻译任务中取得优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的虚拟染色方法高度依赖严格配对且精确配准的训练数据，这些数据在常规实际操作中难以获得且成本高昂。因此迫切需要一个能够降低对配对数据依赖的方法。

Method: 提出了一种半监督学习框架，同时利用有限的配对数据和大量未配对数据。方法核心包括：基于Hessian的形态保持机制，萃取结构线索并约束生成图像保留组织形态；以及病理学真实约束，确保输出更接近真实靶染色图，同时抑制形态和外观漂移。

Result: 在H&E到IHC的Ki67和HER2，以及FFPE到H&E的染色转换任务上，该方法在图像质量、形态保持、鲁棒性和后续诊断性能方面均实现了持续提升。

Conclusion: 所提框架可以有效减少对严格配对数据的依赖，提高虚拟染色的实用性和可靠性，是推动该领域临床应用的关键进展。

Abstract: Virtual staining aims to computationally generate target-stained histopathological images while reducing the cost and time associated with conventional staining procedures. However, existing methods rely predominantly on strictly paired and accurately registered training data, which are difficult and expensive to obtain in routine practice. To reduce this dependence, we propose a stable semi-supervised virtual staining framework that jointly exploits both limited paired data and abundant unpaired source images. Directly incorporating unpaired images is challenging because their generated results lack corresponding targets for supervision, potentially leading to unrealistic staining, morphological degradation, or even training collapse. To obtain reliable supervision from these images, Hessian-derived morphology preservation extracts structural cues from each source image and constrains the generated output to retain tissue morphology. Histopathological realism constraints further guide the output toward plausible target-stain characteristics, preventing the source-derived structural supervision from degenerating into contour enhancement or simple color transformation. Together, the two components suppress structural and appearance drift, stabilize semi-supervised stain translation, and promote the preservation of diagnostically relevant information. Extensive experiments on H&E-to-IHC translation for Ki67 and HER2, as well as FFPE-to-H&E translation, demonstrate consistent improvements in image quality, morphology preservation, robustness, and downstream diagnostic performance. Code will be available.

</details>


### [69] [EvoGS: Modeling Deformation Evolution for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00994)
*Wei Dong,Shahram Shirani,Jun Chen,Han Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoGS动态三维重建框架，通过将高斯变形建模为时序演化过程，提升动态场景新视角合成的质量和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLP的方法对每个时间戳的变形独立估计，导致在处理大幅或突发运动时表现不佳，鲁棒性不足。

Method: EvoGS为每个高斯体维持持续的变形状态，通过历史状态预测未来，并用MLP推断的观测进行校正，校正权重由时序残差记忆和变形速度等统计量自适应调整。另外，提出基于变形的稠密化和不确定性抑制机制，提升了重建质量。

Result: 实验表明，EvoGS在动态新视角合成任务中提升了图像质量，并在多个基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: EvoGS通过引入时序演化建模和自适应校正机制，提高了动态三维重建在大运动场景下的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enable real-time novel view synthesis in dynamic scenes by learning time-conditioned Gaussian deformations. However, existing MLP-based methods typically estimate deformations independently at each timestamp, making them less robust to large or abrupt motions. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{EvoGS}, a 3DGS-based dynamic reconstruction framework that models Gaussian deformation as a temporal evolution process. EvoGS maintains persistent deformation states for each Gaussian, extrapolates future states from historical deformation states, and corrects the predictions with MLP-derived observations. The correction is adaptively weighted using a temporal residual memory and evolution statistics such as deformation velocity and trajectory deviation. To further improve reconstruction quality, EvoGS introduces deformation-aware densification. Clone and split operations are performed along corrected deformation directions, while an uncertainty-aware strategy suppresses densification for Gaussians with unstable deformation histories. Experiments show that EvoGS improves dynamic novel view synthesis quality and achieves competitive performance across benchmarks.

</details>


### [70] [CQF-HMR: Continuous Quaternion Flows for Probabilistic 3D Human Mesh Recovery from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00995)
*Cuong Le,Bao-Long Tran,Pavlo Melnyk,Tahereh Dehdarirad,Bastian Wandt,Mårten Wadenbäck*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用四元数约束的连续归一化流（quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows）的新方法，实现基于单张2D图像的概率型3D人体重建，并在多个基准数据集上达到了最新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单张2D图像恢复3D人体的方法因为深度信息缺失而本质上是病态问题。虽然概率3D姿态估计可以通过生成模型产生多个3D假设以缓解该问题，但现有方法通常只关注3D关键点，忽略了实际应用所需的人体结构一致性。本工作旨在解决姿态估计的合理性和泛化性不足问题。

Method: 作者提出利用四元数表示的连续归一化流生成器，并以2D姿态估计为条件输入。这种新方法结合了SMPL人体参数化模型的身体先验，以及四元数处理关节旋转以避免传统欧拉角的不连续和不可加性问题。

Result: 该方法在Human3.6M数据集上，特别是在歧义环境下取得了最优性能，同时在3DPW和EMDB等具有挑战性的基准测试中实现了可比的姿态估计准确率。

Conclusion: 本文的方法有效提升了基于单张2D图像的3D人体恢复任务中的准确性和合理性，对动画和数字人类等下游应用具有实际价值。

Abstract: Recovering 3D digital humans from a single 2D image is an ill-posed computer vision problem due to the loss of depth information. Probabilistic 3D human pose estimation compensates for this by estimating a set of 3D hypotheses from a prior distribution via generative models. However, most prior work focuses only on 3D keypoints, which often leads to implausible poses that are difficult to apply to downstream tasks, e.g. animation or digital humans. SMPL-based methods are more scalable thanks to the explicit body priors, but it requires more complex modeling of the generation process due to the non-additive nature of the joint rotations. In this work, we propose a novel approach for probabilistic 3D humans using quaternion-constrained continuous normalizing flows conditioned on 2D pose estimations. Our proposed quaternion flows show significant advantages over approaches using other rotation representations. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art results of our method on Human3.6M, particularly in ambiguous settings, and comparable pose estimation accuracy on challenging 3DPW and EMDB benchmarks.

</details>


### [71] [Does This Moment Justify the Recommendation? Counterfactual Behavior-Grounded Evidence Retrieval for Personalized Video Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00996)
*Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将用户行为作为证据的个性化视频推荐证据检索任务，并引入了新数据集CBGER-10K和相应方法CBGER，实现了片段级定位与证据判别的解耦，同时注重于事实与反事实的对比评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化视频推荐方法关注预测用户偏好和定位时间，但很少区分“推荐证据存不存在”与“证据出现在哪里”。作者认为仅有定位（Where）并不能保证定位片段对推荐有效（Whether）。

Method: 作者提出了CBGER-10K数据集，包含5000对事实-反事实视频片段，每对仅改变支持行为的片段而保持其他要素一致；并提出CBGER方法，通过结构化反事实监督分离证据定位与存在性判断同步学习。

Result: CBGER在多个自定义基线任务上取得了0.4432 MRR，0.6977配对准确率和0.6987干预一致性。与QD-DETR相比，MRR无统计显著提升，但配对准确率提升了11.03个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，仅有精准定位未必能获得个性化推荐可靠证据，表明有必要将“哪里”和“是否存在”证据显式区分和评估。

Abstract: Personalized video recommendation predicts user preference at the video level, while temporal video grounding localizes query-relevant moments. However, strong localization does not establish whether the retrieved moment constitutes valid evidence for recommending the video to a particular user. We study counterfactual behavior-grounded evidence retrieval, which separates where personalized evidence occurs from whether such evidence exists and evaluates whether model predictions respond consistently when that evidence is replaced. We introduce CBGER-10K, containing 5,000 controlled factual--counterfactual pairs for 3,026 users, where each pair replaces only the focal behavior-supported segment while preserving the user, temporal position, and hard distractors. We further propose CBGER, a compact framework that decouples segment-level localization from video-level evidence estimation and learns both through structured counterfactual supervision. CBGER achieves $0.4432$ MRR, $0.6977$ Pair Accuracy, and $0.6987$ Intervention Consistency across five adapted personalized-highlight and temporal-grounding baselines. Notably, compared with QD-DETR, its MRR improvement is not statistically significant, while Pair Accuracy improves by $11.03$ points. These results show that accurate temporal localization does not necessarily imply reliable personalized evidence existence, motivating explicit evaluation of Whether alongside Where.

</details>


### [72] [SinkPruner: Sink-Free Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01004)
*Shiyu Li,Zi-Yuan Hu,Shijia Huang,Yanyang Li,Yiwu Zhong,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SinkPruner的新视觉token剪枝框架，可大幅降低多模态大模型（MLLMs）处理长视觉token序列时的计算开销，在显著提升剪枝效果的同时几乎不损失模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs在长视觉token序列推理时计算负担很重，现有视觉或文本引导的剪枝方法常忽视高范数异常token，导致剪枝效果不佳。作者发现这些高范数异常token实际上高度冗余，却常被错误保留。

Method: 提出无需额外训练的SinkPruner，包括两个核心模块：视觉sanitizer（先过滤高范数冗余token，缓解attention sink和attention dispersion问题）和文本引导剪枝器（进一步根据文本语义保留相关token），采用粗到细的设计流程。

Result: 在十二个图文和四个视频任务上大量实验表明，SinkPruner可在减少89%视觉token情况下，保持LLaVA-1.5（Qwen2.5-VL）原始性能的96.5%（91.8%）；其视觉sanitizer还可用于提升现有剪枝方法的表现，转移性良好。

Conclusion: SinkPruner能高效、泛化地降低MLLMs推理所需的token数量，实现大幅加速且性能损失极小，为多模态模型的高效推理提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: Despite their strong multimodal understanding ability, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) incur substantial computational overhead when processing long visual token sequences. To reduce inference costs, recent studies have explored visual token pruning through vision-centric or text-guided strategies. However, these methods often overlook high-norm outlier tokens, i.e., tokens with abnormally large feature norms, leading to suboptimal pruning decisions. In this work, we show that such high-norm outlier tokens are highly redundant in both feature and spatial dimensions, yet are often mistakenly preserved as informative cues by existing methods.
  Motivated by this observation, we propose SinkPruner, a training-free visual token pruning framework for efficient MLLM inference. SinkPruner follows a coarse-to-fine design with two key modules: a visual sanitizer that filters high-norm redundancies and alleviates attention sink and attention dispersion, and a text-guided pruner that further retains tokens semantically aligned with the text query.
  Extensive experiments on twelve image-language and four video-language benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and generalizability of our framework. Notably, SinkPruner preserves 96.5% (91.8%) of the original performance of LLaVA-1.5 (Qwen2.5-VL) under an 89% token reduction. Experiments further indicate that our visual sanitizer exhibits promising transferability in enhancing the performance of existing pruning methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/LaVi-Lab/SinkPruner.

</details>


### [73] [Low-Quality Face Recognition using Center Aligned Representations and Local Margin Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01014)
*Vedat Can Dilaver,Benjamin S. Riggan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的低质量人脸识别（LQFR）方法，有效提升了在不同质量图像上的识别准确率，兼顾了高质量和低质量的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人脸识别在高质量图像下表现优秀，但在低质量图像（如监控视频、人脸模糊或低信噪比）下效果显著下降，同时，仅用低质量数据微调会牺牲高质量泛化，亟需新的解决方法。

Method: 方法整合了三个模块：1）Local Probability Margin（LPM），可基于模型的判别能力估算每个样本的难度；2）嵌套注意力模块（NAM），在部分Transformer层中嵌入一种自注意力机制的低秩适配器；3）质量门控协议（QGP），通过独立的图像质量评估在测试时动态调整适配器的贡献，实现了单一模型对多质量图像的兼容。

Result: 在TinyFace、SurvFace（监控场景），以及IJB-B、IJB-C（标准场景）等多个基准数据集上，所提方法在人脸识别和验证任务中取得了稳定且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能更好地在全质量范围内的人脸识别中权衡性能，既提升了低质量下的识别能力，又不影响高质量的人脸识别表现，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Low-quality face recognition (LQFR) remains challenging due to the difficulty of matching degraded query (probe) images against low-quality (LQ) enrollment (gallery) imagery and the scarcity of training data for large-scale models. While recent face recognition (FR) models perform well on high-quality (HQ) imagery, their accuracy drops significantly on LQ images with extremely low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Moreover, fine-tuning HQ-pretrained models on LQ data often improves LQ recognition at the expense of HQ generalization. This trade-off becomes more pronounced in modern evaluation settings spanning multiple datasets with varying image quality levels. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that combines three main components: (1) Local Probability Margin (LPM), which estimates per-sample difficulty directly from the model's discriminative landscape; (2) Nested Attention Module (NAM), a new low-rank adapter module that embeds a self-attention mechanism within selected transformer layers; and (3) Quality Gating Protocol (QGP), where an off-the-shelf image quality estimator modulates the adapter contribution at test time, enabling a single model to handle the full quality spectrum without sacrificing HQ performance. Experiments on surveillance (TinyFace, SurvFace) and standard (IJB-B, IJB-C) face recognition benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains in both identification and verification. Code and models will be released at github.com/candllq/nam.

</details>


### [74] [Fi-ImageNet-1k: An OOD Benchmark From the Inside of the ImageNet-1k Validation Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01027)
*Ruslan Rozumnyi,Matěj Suchánek,Tomáš Vojíř,Klára Janoušková,Jiří Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fi-ImageNet-1k，作为更加具挑战性的OOD（分布外）检测基准数据集。它从ImageNet-1k的人类重注释标记错误图片中筛选出来，经过专家和多模型比对，专门收集了不属于ImageNet-1k任何类别，但有确切类别归属的图片，共含655张图片、522种类别。实验表明，现有OOD检测方法在该数据集上的表现远不如其他数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布外检测基准通常依赖随意收集的分布外图片，缺乏严格标准和挑战性。作者发现，ID（分布内）标签数据集本身因标签错误就是天然的OOD来源，利用这一现象可以构建更具代表性和难度的OOD数据集。

Method: 作者利用ReImageNet的人类重注释结果，从ImageNet-1k验证集选出所有未被归入ImageNet-1k已知类别的图片。每张图片都经专家与多模态模型（MLLMs、VLMs）和反向图片搜索的筛查，确保只保留属于ImageNet-1k类外、且有明确具体类别的图片，最终构建了Fi-ImageNet-1k。

Result: Fi-ImageNet-1k包含655张图片、522个类别，比现有最具挑战性的NINCO数据集在最先进的OOD检测方法下FPR@95指标上难3.8倍。所有评测组合的FPR@95都不低于51%，明显高于基准数据集。

Conclusion: Fi-ImageNet-1k为OOD检测提供了前所未有的挑战性和更严格的评测标准，能够促进更有效、鲁棒的分布外检测方法的研究与发展。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection predicts whether a test image belongs to none of the predefined classes. To evaluate this task, benchmarks need images from outside the in-distribution (ID) data; typically, these are defined or collected in an ad hoc fashion. Since no ground truth is perfect, ID-labeled datasets themselves contain a natural source of OOD images. We exploit such annotation errors and present Fi-ImageNet-1k, an OOD dataset built from ImageNet-1k validation images that the recent ReImageNet reannotation effort assigned to no ImageNet-1k class. Each image was examined by expert human annotators supported by evidence from MLLMs, VLMs, and reverse image search, comparing it against all visually similar ID classes. We keep only images that could be assigned a specific class outside the ImageNet-1k label space.
  The resulting Fi-ImageNet-1k, with 655 images from 522 classes, is substantially more challenging than any commonly used OOD dataset. No evaluated combination of classifier and OOD detector achieves a false positive rate below 51% at 95% true positive rate (FPR@95). Compared to the recent NINCO, our dataset is 3.8x more challenging in the FPR@95 metric for state-of-the-art supervised OOD detection methods.

</details>


### [75] [ViTAMINS: An Empirical Study of Training Self-Supervised Vision Transformers with Synthetic Hard Negatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01041)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Andreas Floros,Kleanthis-Marios Papadopoulos,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViTAMINS 通过在无监督视觉Transformer预训练中引入合成困难负样本，有效提升了表示质量，并在多个视觉任务中表现优异，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对比学习虽有效，但生成主义和自蒸馏方法逐渐占主导。论文旨在探索一种更简单高效的改进方案，通过引入合成难负样本提升表示能力与下游任务表现。

Method: 提出ViTAMINS方法，在现有对比框架基础上，融入合成的困难负样本进行无监督预训练。对该方法在ImageNet分类、迁移学习、图像检索、拷贝检测、图像与视频分割等任务中进行系统评测。

Result: ViTAMINS方法得到的表示不仅包含明确的语义信息，其用于分类的精度较现有基线最高提升达11.3%。同等资源下，ViT-B规模即超越V-JEPA的ViT-L。表现强于各类主流方法，且更资源高效。

Conclusion: 简单地在对比学习中加入合成难负样本，不仅提升了表示质量和下游任务效果，还显示出对生成式与自蒸馏主流方法的资源效率和性能优势。建议重新审视对比学习作为高效的表示学习框架。

Abstract: We introduce ViTAMINS, a method that integrates synthetic hard negatives into unsupervised vision transformer pretraining to improve representation quality. Our approach is thoroughly benchmarked on ImageNet and transfer learning, image retrieval, copy detection, and image, video segmentation tasks. Notably, our proposed negatives give rise to emergent properties, where learned representations contain explicit information about the semantic content of an image and serve as excellent classifiers (up to +11.3% over baselines). ViTAMINS achieves these benefits through simple modifications to existing contrastive frameworks and outperforms competing methods while being more resource efficient, e.g., our ViT-B surpasses V-JEPA with ViT-L. Our findings motivate reconsidering contrastive learning as a simpler yet powerful alternative to dominant generative and self-distillation approaches.

</details>


### [76] [Dyn-3D: Unveiling and Resolving Ego-Motion Ambiguity in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01059)
*Jiayu Ding,Zhuodong Liu,Lei Zhang,Manyu Xiong,Hongbo Jin,Haoran Tang,Hongbo Zhang,Changen Zhu,Wenbo Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）在处理动态3D空间推理时存在的运动感知缺陷，命名为Kinematic Collapse，并提出了新的评测基准Dyn-3D及改进方法TempoVista，有效提升了运动估计和空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM虽然能处理多模态任务，但其空间推理能力在大范围运动场景下表现不佳，主要因为过拟合轨迹先验而非真实物理运动，该研究旨在解决由于视频数据固有限制和缺少物理监督引发的推理退化问题。

Method: (1) 提出Dyn-3D基准，利用反事实3D渲染准确区分外观变化与真实运动属性。
(2) 提出TempoVista框架，包含Kinematic-GSPO算法，将物理地面真实数据引入策略优化，将视觉表征与3D空间运动显式绑定。

Result: 实验表明，TempoVista显著改善了动态场景下运动估计及空间推理表现，利用相机运动作为几何校准信号带来更稳健的推理能力。

Conclusion: 引入物理监督和新的评测方式，有效缓解了VLM模型在处理大幅运动时的退化问题，为后续3D视觉理解和多模态推理研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: As Vision-Language Models (VLMs) tackle dynamic 3D spatial reasoning, ego-motion perception becomes essential to resolve monocular scale ambiguity. However, current models often overfit to smooth trajectory priors rather than genuinely understanding physical motion. Consequently, their spatial reasoning degrades severely under large displacements, a phenomenon we term Kinematic Collapse. This failure stems from spurious visual-motion correlations in natural videos and a lack of explicit physical supervision. To evaluate this, we introduce Dyn-3D, a benchmark using counterfactual 3D rendering to rigorously decouple visual changes from true kinematic properties. Furthermore, we propose the TempoVista framework, featuring the Kinematic-GSPO algorithm. By embedding metric physical ground truth into policy optimization, TempoVista explicitly grounds visual representations in 3D space. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves both motion estimation and robust spatial reasoning by utilizing camera dynamics as an effective geometric calibration signal.

</details>


### [77] [IT-TextFusion: Iterative Text-Image Interaction with Text-Guided Residual Refinement for Degradation-Aware Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01092)
*Siyang Liu,Peiyi Zhou,Tianle Jin,Rongrong Bian,Zheke Jin,Mengze Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代式文本引导图像融合框架，显著提升了复杂退化场景下多模态信息的融合效果，并在多个基准数据集上证明了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导的融合方法在语义-视觉交互和注意力机制上较为浅显，难以应对复杂的图像退化情况，也无法充分利用文本信息进行更精细的融合控制。

Method: 方法上，作者设计了一个迭代式的图像融合框架，融合了深层次的Cross-Attention、多尺度Cross-Gate Fusion和分阶段基于文本的调制机制。全局文本嵌入被多次注入到解码和细化各个阶段，实现分层特征融合与残差优化，并强化文本对全流程的引导。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多个主流数据集上取得了多项信息保留和感知质量指标的提升，不过在部分数据集上不同指标之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在保障多模态互补信息、提升复杂退化适应性的同时，实现了更优信息保留和感知质量，展示了文本引导图像融合的新优势。

Abstract: Text-guided image fusion has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for integrating multi-modal information while enabling flexible and task-oriented fusion control. However, existing text-guided fusion methods often rely on shallow semantic-visual interaction and limited attention mechanisms, which restrict their ability to robustly handle complex degradations and fully exploit textual guidance. In this paper, we propose an iterative text-guided image fusion framework that incorporates text-conditioned feature interaction across multiple fusion and refinement stages. The proposed method integrates deepest-level Cross-Attention, multi-scale Cross-Gate Fusion, and stage-specific text-conditioned modulation, allowing the global text embedding to condition hierarchical feature fusion and residual refinement. By repeatedly injecting the pooled text embedding across hierarchical decoder and refinement stages, the proposed framework provides degradation-aware global semantic conditioning while preserving complementary information from the visible and infrared modalities. Experiments on several benchmark datasets show that the proposed method improves several information-preservation and perceptual-quality metrics, while exhibiting metric-dependent trade-offs on some datasets.

</details>


### [78] [P-PatchDiff: Progressive Patch Diffusion Models for Low-light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01123)
*Ruoyu Guo,Haonan Zhong,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了P-PatchDiff，一种用于低光照图像增强的渐进式可扩展Patch扩散框架。该方法解决了传统Patch扩散模型难以兼顾图像级亮度上下文与效率的问题，实现了自适应多尺度增强，并大幅提升了运算速度和内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Patch扩散模型通常使用固定小块（如64x64），难以获取全图亮度信息，增大感受野虽能改善亮度和色彩估计，但显著增加计算资源消耗。同时，低光图像区域亮度分布不均，拼接后难以保证整体亮度一致性。因此需要一种兼顾局部-全局、多尺度、亮度一致的新方法。

Method: 提出渐进式Patch扩散（P-PatchDiff）框架，在扩散去噪过程中动态调整Patch大小，实现从局部到全局的逐步信息融合。引入多Patch对齐策略，利用全局亮度代理对不同Patch尺度的特征进行标准化，提升多区域亮度一致性。同时方法对可扩展性和效率进行了专门设计。

Result: P-PatchDiff可有效增强分辨率从400x600到4K的低光图像。与现有Patch扩散方法相比，速度提升80倍，内存占用低于9GB，保证了高效、可扩展的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过渐进式、多尺度动态Patch和多Patch对齐策略，实现了更好的亮度一致性和全局信息利用。P-PatchDiff在实际低光增强任务中表现优异，具备实用意义与推广价值。

Abstract: Recent advancements in low-light image enhancement have leveraged diffusion models for their strong ability to generate perceptually realistic, detailed images. Patch diffusion models further offer a promising solution to size-agnostic image restoration while improving efficiency. However, existing methods typically rely on small, fixed patches (e.g., 64$\times$64) that cannot capture image-level brightness context, whereas enlarging the receptive field improves brightness and colour estimation but substantially increases computational cost. Moreover, low-light images often exhibit uneven brightness across regions, making it necessary to ensure that locally enhanced patches remain visually coherent when combined into the full image. To address these limitations, we propose P-PatchDiff, a scalable progressive patch diffusion framework for low-light image enhancement that dynamically adjusts patch size throughout the denoising process, enabling a gradual shift from local to global views. A Multi-Patch Alignment strategy is also introduced to normalise features across varying patch scales using an estimated global brightness proxy. Rather than pursuing pixel-level reconstruction accuracy, P-PatchDiff focuses on scalability and coherent brightness across the whole image, allowing the model to perceive multi-scale information and better enhance regions with varying brightness. We empirically demonstrate that P-PatchDiff effectively enhances images ranging from 400 $\times$ 600 to 4K and is 80$\times$ faster than existing patch diffusion models while using less than 9GB of memory. The code is available at https://github.com/RuoyuGuo/P-PatchDiff.

</details>


### [79] [Different Changes Require Different Reasoning: Change-Type-Specialized Experts for Robust Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01136)
*Jiyoung Park,InJae Oh,Jung Uk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种多专家诊断（MEDIC）框架，通过引入变更类型感知机制，提升了图像变更描述任务的性能。该方法在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往方法忽略了不同类型图像变更的区别，未能针对颜色变化、物体新增等不同变更类型采用专门机制，影响了变更描述的准确性和细粒度。

Method: 提出MEDIC框架，利用类型专门化的记忆专家，针对不同变更类型建模，动态地提取与变更类型相关的视觉模式。通过输入在专家间的软路由机制，学习每种变更的专有表示，提升描述能力。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的数据集上，MEDIC均显著优于现有基线方法，验证了类型感知机制和多专家架构的有效性。

Conclusion: MEDIC框架能生成更准确、类型敏感的图像变更描述，为后续变更理解和描述研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Change captioning is the task of generating natural language descriptions that explain the changes between a pair of images. Although different change types (e.g., color shifts, object additions) exhibit distinct visual cues and require specialized reasoning processes, existing methods often overlook these distinctions. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Expert Diagnosis for Image Change (MEDIC), a novel framework that introduces change-type awareness by explicitly modeling change categories. MEDIC employs type-specialized memory experts that dynamically retrieve type-relevant visual patterns conditioned on the input. This design enables each expert to capture diverse variations within its change type while focusing on the most informative visual cues. By softly routing inputs across type-specialized experts and learning dedicated representations for each change category, MEDIC generates more precise and type-aware change descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed MEDIC consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse and challenging datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/VisualAIKHU/MEDIC}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [80] [Revisiting Face Recognition for Monozygotic Twins: The Celeb Twins Test Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01141)
*Michael Zang,Haiyu Wu,Mrinal Sharma,Kevin W. Bowyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的名人双胞胎面部识别测试集（CTTS），并探讨如何利用面部标记和镜像不对称性提高同卵双胞胎的识别准确率。作者还研究了现有深度CNN匹配器的表现及其局限，并讨论了通过生成式AI工具扩展双胞胎样本的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同卵双胞胎的面部特征极其相似，现有的人脸识别技术对他们的区分能力有限。提升双胞胎识别的准确率并丰富相关数据集对于推动该领域发展具有重要意义。

Method: 作者构建了包含80对名人双胞胎的CTTS测试集，并为其中区分性强的皮肤标记和可能存在镜像不对称的双胞胎配备元数据。采用当前主流的深度CNN匹配器对CTTS进行人脸验证实验，并分析这些方法是否利用了皮肤标记或对称性信息。最后，作者探讨了采用生成式AI生成虚拟双胞胎图像扩展训练集的可能性。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有的深度CNN匹配器在CTTS数据集上同人/不同人识别准确率可达76%以上，但没有有效利用皮肤标记或者不对称性信息。

Conclusion: 双胞胎识别中，面部细节如皮肤标记和对称性尚未被主流方法有效利用。通过引入元数据和生成式AI合成图像，有可能提升双胞胎识别模型的表现和数据多样性。

Abstract: Past literature on face recognition for monozygotic (("identical") twins points to facial marks and mirror asymmetry as possible directions for improved accuracy of twins recognition. The Celeb Twins Test Set (CTTS) contains web-scraped image pairs for 80 sets of celebrity twins. It is the only twins test set with meta-data for twins with distinguishing skin marks and possible mirror asymmetry. CTTS is organized in the manner of face verification test sets such as LFW, CALFW, CPLFW, CFP-FP, and AgeDB-30. Current deep CNN matchers can achieve over 76% accuracy in classifying CTTS same-person / different-person image pairs. We show that current matchers do not make use of skin marks, or asymmetry, and discuss reasons for this. Finally, we discuss the feasibility of using generative AI tools such as Grok, ChatGPT and Gemini to create images of imagined monozygotic twins as a means to increase representation of twins in face recognition training sets.

</details>


### [81] [StainPresetNet: Stain Preset Network for Fast Multi-to-Multi Stain Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01146)
*Hongtao Kang,Die Luo,Li Chen,Jing Cai,Junbo Hu,Xiuli Liu,Shenghua Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型染色标准化方法StainPresetNet，结合结构保留与高效的像素级标准化，显著提升色彩映射准确性、诊断分类泛化能力，并大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统染色标准化方法色彩提取准确度低，深度学习方法虽准却算子复杂、低效且方向固定，不便实际应用。

Method: 提出StainPresetNet，融合结构保留及基于预置参考图像的像素级归一化，支持多方向且高效、无需重训练的染色标准化。

Result: 在细胞学和组织学数据集上的实验证明，StainPresetNet色彩映射的准确性优于传统方法，分类器泛化能力提升，计算开销较主流深度学习方法降低90%。

Conclusion: StainPresetNet在保持结构和高效性的前提下，能灵活切换标准化方向，显著克服现有方法局限，或将推动自动化病理诊断的发展。

Abstract: Stain normalization reduces color variations caused by variations in staining protocols and imaging conditions, thereby enhancing computer-aided diagnostic system performance. Traditional methods derive mapping relationships from individual or limited reference images through pixel-wise transformation, offering style flexibility but suffering from inaccurate color mapping extraction. While existing deep-learning-based approaches achieve accurate dataset-wide color mapping through complex neural networks, they face challenges including computational inefficiency, artifact generation, and fixed normalization directions requiring model retraining for directional changes. To address these limitations, we propose StainPresetNet - a novel framework that combines structural preservation with dataset-level color mapping while maintaining computational efficiency. Our method implements pixel-wise normalization guided by preset reference images, enabling multi-directional adaptability without retraining. Evaluations on cytopathology and histopathology datasets demonstrate that StainPresetNet achieves superior color mapping accuracy compared to conventional methods, effectively improves classifier generalization in diagnostic tasks, and reduces computational overhead by 90\% versus existing deep learning approaches. The proposed preset-guided mechanism facilitates flexible adjustment of normalization directions through simple reference image replacement, overcoming the directional rigidity of current deep-learning-based solutions.

</details>


### [82] [On the Design Fundamentals of Pixel Text Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01147)
*Chaohao Yuan,Ruifeng Yuan,Zhuoxu Huang,Yu Rong,Hong Cheng,Hou Pong Chan,Chenghao Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Pixel Linguist II，一种用于文本丰富视觉输入的视觉编码器，解决了以往像素-文本模型在分辨率、视觉捷径、多语言理解等方面的难题，取得多项SOTA表现，支持高效文本压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的像素-文本编码器难以应对多语言、多分辨率与文本在像素空间的复杂排列，导致理解与压缩效果有限，亟需新的设计原则提升文本视觉输入的表征能力。

Method: 通过系统性消融实验，作者确立了四个核心组件：多分辨率及字体渲染实现高分辨率泛化；天然图文对提升视觉锚定避免文本拟合崩溃；布局感知渲染防止利用图像捷径；两阶段多语课程实现跨语对齐。综合四大设计，通过在线渲染与统一对比学习，在2.8亿样本规模下训练出Pixel Linguist II模型。

Result: Pixel Linguist II在英语、跨语言和多语视觉语义文本相似度（Visual STS）与ViDoRe任务上均取得SOTA成绩，还提升了多模态大模型评测性能。在80%视觉令牌压缩下仍具鲁棒性，展现了极高的上下文压缩潜力。

Conclusion: 本文为视觉文本表征建模提供了清晰设计范式，并通过大规模实验证明其有效性，推动了多语言、多场景下的图文理解与压缩领域发展。

Abstract: Text-rich visual inputs require models that can read, retrieve, and compress language directly in pixel space, yet existing pixel-text encoders struggle with fixed resolution pretraining, visual shortcut learning, weak visual grounding, and multilingual visual text understanding. In this work, we investigate the fundamental design principles required for robust visual text representation learning. Through systematic controlled ablations, we identify four critical components: variable image resolutions and rendered font sizes provide spatial proxies for high-resolution document generalization; natural image-text pairs are indispensable for grounding and prevent text-only collapse; layout-aware rendering helps prevent pixel-level shortcuts; and a two-stage multilingual curriculum enables effective cross-lingual alignment. By integrating these principles into a scalable training recipe, we train Pixel Linguist II, a native-resolution vision encoder trained with on-the-fly rendering, unified contrastive grounding, and a multilingual curriculum over 280M training examples. Pixel Linguist II sets new state-of-the-art results on English, cross-lingual, and multilingual Visual STS and ViDoRe, while also enabling better MLLM downstream evaluation. Notably, Pixel Linguist II remains robust under 80\% visual token compression, showing great promise for optical context compression. Our code and resources are available at https://github.com/Pixel-Linguist/Pixel-Linguist-II.

</details>


### [83] [Dotting the Eye: An Intent-Driven Image Retouching Agent for Visual Focus Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01148)
*Chujie Qin,Zilong Zhang,Zewei Chang,Chunle Guo,Ruixing Wang,Tao Hu,Ming-Ming Cheng,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为EyeControl的系统，通过少量用户输入即可实现图片视觉焦点增强，显著提升用户对图像局部区域的关注，并保证整体自然美观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像调色不仅仅提升视觉质量，还常常用于引导观看者注意特定对象或区域。传统实现这一目标需要专业技能，并且很难同时协调全局与局部的调整。为简化操作并提高效果，亟需便捷且智能的自动化视觉焦点增强方法。

Method: 作者提出EyeControl系统，核心在于将用户弱意图（如点击或粗略画线）与目标编辑区域和相应色调调整操作显式关联。系统包括MLLM（多模态大模型）驱动的意图解析器，以及基于扩散模型的自动调色执行体。通过意图解析，系统推断目标焦点并输出结构化引导给执行体，后者采用伪意图图和操作一致性约束，实现全局与局部调整的自然衔接。

Result: 作者构建了高质量评测数据集ControlArt-Bench，并进行了大量实验。结果表明，EyeControl能有效突出指定区域，增强视觉意图一致性，并在主观感知上取得更高评价。相关代码开源。

Conclusion: EyeControl实现了面向视觉焦点增强的智能图像调色，在用户仅需简单操作的情况下，实现专业级效果，自然美观且意图响应强，具有较强的实用价值。

Abstract: Image retouching is commonly formulated as enhancing overall visual quality through color adjustment, but in practice, it also serves to emphasize visual focus by guiding viewers' attention toward a specific subject or region. Achieving such focus-oriented retouching is inherently challenging, as it requires well-coordinated global and local adjustments to manipulate perceptual saliency while maintaining visual naturalness. This intricate process typically demands substantial professional expertise. In this study, we propose EyeControl, a MLLM-driven agent with a diffusion-based retouching executor that enables visual focus enhancement under weak user intent. With only a few clicks or coarse strokes, EyeControl directs visual attention to the intended region, effectively "dotting the eye" of the image. The core idea is to explicitly link the weak user intention with the target editing region and the corresponding tonal adjustment operations during retouching. To achieve this, the system first interprets the intent and image content to infer the visual focus and generate structured intent guidance for the retouching executor. Second, the retouching executor is encouraged to respond more strongly to the target region, explicitly aligning its attention map with a designed pseudo-intent map. We also introduce an operation-consistency constraint to improve coordination between global and local adjustments, achieving more natural and coherent retouching. Additionally, we contribute ControlArt-Bench, a high-quality evaluation dataset for visual focus enhancement. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that EyeControl yields perceptually appealing results with stronger intent alignment. Code can be found in https://github.com/DragonisCV/EyeControl.

</details>


### [84] [Monocular Depth Estimation from a Single Image: Progress and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01172)
*Muxin Liu,Xiaoyang Lyu,Yang-Tian Sun,Yi-Hua Huang,Ziyi Yang,Peng Dai,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于单目深度估计领域的综述，从早期方法到基础大模型的最新进展，系统梳理了该领域的发展历程、主要方法、数据集、实际应用及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计是计算机视觉中的核心难题，广泛应用于3D重建、机器人、自动驾驶和增强现实。本综述旨在总结该领域从早期学习方法到基础大模型的演变，帮助研究人员更好地把握技术脉络和挑战。

Method: 首先梳理问题类型（相对/绝对深度估计）及主要挑战，然后介绍常见问题定义和主流数据集（室内、室外及合成数据），回顾基础大模型前的关键方法。随后，系统总结近年来基于基础大模型的方法，通过区分判别式和生成式，并强调大规模预训练（如DINOv3）和合成数据的作用，结合定量和定性分析，对主要模型进行比较。此外，还扩展讨论了基于视频的深度估计及其在视觉SLAM、内容生成、机器人感知等现实应用中的集成。

Result: 阐述了各类单目深度估计算法从精度、效率和鲁棒性等方面的进展，比较了基于传统深度学习和基础大模型的方法表现，总结了相关领域在应用中的实际效果，并指明了基础大模型加速领域发展的趋势。

Conclusion: 基础大模型驱动的单目深度估计取得了显著进展，但在泛化能力、数据集构建、实时性和特定场景适应性等方面仍面临挑战。未来应关注更高效的模型设计、多模态融合、合成与真实数据协同、以及面向实际应用的系统集成。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has long stood as a fundamental challenge in computer vision, enabling a wide range of applications including 3D reconstruction, robotics, autonomous driving, and augmented reality. This survey traces the field's evolution from early learning-based methods to the emergence of transformative foundation models. We begin by framing the problem, distinguishing between relative and metric depth estimation, and highlighting the key challenges that have shaped a decade of research. We then present common problem formulations and introduce the most widely used datasets, covering indoor, outdoor, and synthetic data. Following this, we review major advances prior to the foundation model era, distilling core insights from influential methods that contributed to improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and robustness. The survey then turns to the recent surge of foundation-model-based approaches, categorizing them into discriminative and generative paradigms and emphasizing the critical roles of large-scale pretraining (e.g., DINOv3) and synthetic data. We compare representative models using both quantitative benchmarks and qualitative examples, and discuss natural extensions to video-based depth estimation. Further, to illustrate real-world impact, we highlight the integration of depth estimation into applications such as visual SLAM, content generation, and robot perception. Finally, we outline open challenges and promising research directions as the field advances further into the era of foundation models.

</details>


### [85] [Compressing AI Traffic: Standardized Neural Network Coding of Visual-Token Representations in Split Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01200)
*Reza Heidari,Hamed R. Tavakoli,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了视觉-语言模型在分布式部署时，视觉编码器与语言解码器之间中间张量（AI traffic）的压缩潜力。实验证明，即使大幅压缩视觉token，模型性能几乎不受影响，直至压缩率极高才性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分布式AI推理场景增多，不同模块往往运行于不同计算节点间，因此中间激活张量需跨节点传输。这引发了AI traffic的高效压缩需求，以减少带宽消耗、降低推理成本。作者希望评估无需训练的新型张量压缩标准（NNC）对实际VLM任务的可用性和影响。

Method: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct视频问答流程中，将ISO/IEC 15938-17神经网络编码（NNC）嵌入模型视觉接口，对视觉token表示及特征流进行有损压缩。调整量化参数，系统性测试模型在不同压缩率（码率）下的性能，仅保留主干结构，不改变模型权重和推理设置。

Result: 实验证明，视觉主token传输量减少98%时，模型在闭合式与开放式视频理解任务的准确率几乎不变，仅在极限高压缩下性能骤降。压缩张量存在明显信息损失，但模型下游推理对精确数值并不敏感，而更依赖于结构信息。

Conclusion: AI流量的压缩无需追求极小失真，模型鲁棒性基于结构和相对几何特征，对精确还原不敏感。因此，AI traffic的压缩标准应以任务性能为优化目标（率-任务优化），而非传统的信号还原质量（率-失真指标）。

Abstract: When the visual encoder and the language decoder of a vision-language model (VLM) run on different compute nodes, the intermediate visual-token embeddings become a communicated payload rather than an internal activation. We call such machine-consumed intermediate tensors AI traffic and ask how far they can be compressed with a standardized, training-free codec. We insert ISO/IEC 15938-17 Neural Network Coding (NNC) round trips on the complete visual interface of a Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct video question answering pipeline, comprising the main visual-token representation and the DeepStack feature streams, while leaving weights, prompts, and generation untouched, and sweep the quantization parameter (QP) over a wide rate range. Closed-ended Video-MME accuracy remains close to the uncompressed reference up to a 98% reduction of the transmitted BF16 tensor and only then collapses; open-ended MLVU generation shows the same plateau-and-collapse profile under an LLM judge. This robustness is not due to near-lossless reconstruction: the decoded tensor is heavily discretized, carries substantial row-wise relative L2 error, and has a visibly steeper singular-value decay than its source. Downstream reasoning therefore depends on coarse structure and relative geometry rather than exact floating-point values, which argues for rate-task rather than rate-distortion optimization of AI traffic codecs.

</details>


### [86] [S$^2$Prune: Spatially Structured Visual Token Pruning for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01224)
*Yuanyuan Jia,Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出S$^2$Prune，一种无训练的视觉token剪枝方法，能在多模态大模型中大幅降低推理代价，同时维持视觉空间覆盖，并自适应图像局部结构分配token密度，在极少token量下保持高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法常基于重要性或冗余选择token，导致空间偏差明显，且不总是优于简单均匀采样，说明良好的空间覆盖同样重要。为改进此点，作者提出需兼顾空间覆盖及图像结构感知的剪枝策略。

Method: S$^2$Prune首先将图像分区，对每区至少分配一个token确保空间覆盖，剩余token则依据区域的Laplacian变差（结构丰富度）分配；区域内用Early Representation Change（ERC）从第一个解码器块中选取代表token。

Result: 在多种设置和两个多模态大模型（MLLM）上评测S$^2$Prune，尤其是在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型上，其在所有无训练剪枝方法中准确率最高，仅用32个token（原始576个的5.5%）保留了79.3%的完整性能。

Conclusion: S$^2$Prune在大幅减少视觉token数量和计算量的同时，有效保留关键空间与结构信息，是无需训练的高效视觉token剪枝方法，对提升MLLMs推理效率具重要价值。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference overhead of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by retaining only a subset of visual tokens. Existing methods usually select tokens based on importance or redundancy. However, we observe that these criteria produce stable spatial biases across inputs and do not always outperform simple Uniform Grid sampling, highlighting the value of broad spatial coverage. Motivated by this, we propose S$^2$Prune, a training-free pruning method that preserves spatial coverage while adapting token density to local image structure. We first divide the image into regions and assign at least one token to each region to preserve coverage. The remaining token budget is then distributed according to Laplacian variation, giving more tokens to regions with richer structure. We then use Early Representation Change (ERC), computed from the first decoder block, to select representative tokens within each region. We evaluate S$^2$Prune across diverse settings and two MLLM architectures. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, it achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated training-free pruning methods. With only 32 of the original 576 visual tokens, it still retains 79.3% of the full-model performance. Code is available at https://github.com/yuanyuanjia71-spec/S2Prune.

</details>


### [87] [One Prompt Is Enough: Watermark Laundering Through Foundation Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01249)
*Jidong Yang,Qi Li,Wei Zong,Yang-Wai Chow,Willy Susilo,Huaike Yu,Chunpeng Wang,Suo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现，当前一般用来检验隐形水印鲁棒性的测试（如压缩、模糊等）不足以应对大模型的“重建攻击”，即攻击者通过重建提示词让大模型输出几乎一致但水印已被删除的新图像，对水印方案构成严重威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式大模型（如OpenAI、Google的图像模型）的普及，攻击者可以通过文本提示让模型“重建”带水印的图片，而传统的图像处理扰动检测方式无法评估模型重建带来的水印失效问题。因此，需要分析并扩展水印机制对大模型攻击的鲁棒性标准。

Method: 作者将这种攻击正式定义为“水印漂白（laundering）”，并提出了结合位错误率（BER）、图像视觉和语义保持性的联合评估方法。论文选择了6种主流的OpenAI与Google图像模型、3个具有代表性的隐形水印方案，并在1800组重建输出上进行了深入实验分析，辨析了重建攻击的不同表现和机制。

Result: OpenAI的模型在删除原水印信息上更彻底，即使保持视觉与语义一致性时，水印也难以被还原；Nano Banana 2模型则显示某些水印算法（如DwtDct）在“高保真”重建中同样脆弱。此外，分析提示词发现，水印失效主要来自模型的重建机制而非显式的攻击提示；与传统攻击方式（如压缩噪声）比较，提示词重建路径成为一种全新的有效攻击渠道。

Conclusion: 隐形水印的鲁棒性评测亟需纳入大模型重建这一新型攻击视角，现有方法面对这种攻击尚存漏洞。未来的水印设计与评估需针对模型重建进行补强与提升。

Abstract: Invisible watermarks are typically evaluated against predefined perturbations such as compression, blur, noise, cropping, and denoising. Public foundation image models expose a distinct threat: an attacker can submit a watermarked image with a single reconstruction prompt and obtain a visually faithful output from which the invisible watermark can no longer be decoded reliably. We formalize this failure mode as watermark laundering and evaluate it using a joint payload-fidelity profile that combines bit error rate (BER) with visual and semantic preservation. Across six OpenAI and Google image editing models, three representative watermarking schemes, and 1,800 reconstructed outputs, we identify two complementary laundering regimes: OpenAI models produce the strongest payload disruption across the evaluated schemes, whereas Nano Banana 2 shows that DwtDct remains vulnerable under high-fidelity reconstruction. Prompt ablations show that no single removal-oriented instruction is necessary for payload disruption, indicating that the effect is primarily induced by the reconstruction pathway rather than by explicit attack wording. Comparisons with conventional attacks further show that prompt-conditioned reconstruction constitutes a distinct operational attack interface. These findings motivate foundation-model reconstruction as a missing robustness condition in invisible watermark evaluation.

</details>


### [88] [Seeing the World and the Self from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01276)
*Kai Guan,Minchao Jiang,Ruichen WangLi,Wentao Zhu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出RESELF框架，用于从第一视角视频中联合重建三维场景和穿戴者全身运动，在多项任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常分别处理场景重建和人体动作估计，两者之间缺乏协同，且存在可见性不对称与不同预测范式的挑战。该论文提出联合恢复来提升整体三维感知能力。

Method: RESELF联合基于几何的场景重建和基于扩散模型的人体运动生成，通过从大规模第三视角数据预训练的基础几何模型进行自适应，结合尺度与相对位姿一致性目标，引入闭环运动反馈进一步优化摄像机轨迹；并新整理EE4D-JSM数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: RESELF在深度估计、摄像机跟踪和全身运动估计等任务上，在EE4D-JSM等数据集上均超越各单项任务的最新方法。

Conclusion: 联合三维场景与自身体运动重建能够提升第一视角视频的整体三维感知表现，该框架为后续相关研究提供了数据和方法基础。

Abstract: Complete 3D perception from egocentric video requires recovering the surrounding scene and the wearer's full-body motion in a shared metric frame. Existing methods typically address scene reconstruction and motion estimation separately: scene reconstruction methods ignore the wearer, whereas motion estimation methods lack explicit scene geometry and often depend on external trajectories. Joint recovery is challenging because the two tasks exhibit asymmetric visibility and require different prediction paradigms. The largely visible scene supports deterministic geometric regression, whereas the severely occluded body requires generative motion inference. We therefore propose RESELF (REconstructing the Scene and the sELF), a unified framework that couples deterministic metric geometry reconstruction with geometry-conditioned motion generation. RESELF adapts a geometry foundation model pre-trained on large-scale exocentric data to egocentric video using frame-wise scale and relative-pose consistency objectives. The resulting camera trajectory and latent geometric features condition a diffusion model that recovers the wearer's motion. A subsequent closed-loop kinematic feedback stage further refines the camera head while preserving the reconstructed scene geometry. To support training and evaluation, we curate EE4D-JSM from EgoExo4D by aligning egocentric video, sparse metric scene geometry, camera trajectories, and full-body motion annotations. Experiments show that RESELF outperforms state-of-the-art methods designed for the individual tasks across depth estimation, camera tracking, and full-body motion estimation. Code, models, and datasets will be available at https://ka1guan.github.io/RESELF/.

</details>


### [89] [TimeSteer: Inference-Time Speech Scheduling in Joint Audio-Visual Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01277)
*Chao Zhou,Yiling Chen,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Tianyi We*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练，只基于推理阶段即可实现语音-视频内容在用户指定时间区间内精确控制的新框架TimeSteer，并首次推出相应基准SpeechShift。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前预训练的音频-视觉扩散模型虽然对内容"生成什么"有很大控制，但无法精确控制"何时"发生，例如指定一段语音在某个时间段出现，这对于实际应用非常重要。

Method: 分析去噪过程中的两个固有特性：1）时序敏感的跨模态注意力头可揭示语音片段的潜在时域范围；2）模型生成的潜在空间已包含语音与视觉耦合的时序关系。基于此，提出TimeSteer框架，包括Source Span Localization（定位片段时域范围）与Region-Aware Latent Remapping（将内容在潜在空间内从源区间映射到目标区间），完全无需对主干网络进行微调。同时，提出SpeechShift基准，评价生成模型区间层面的控制能力。

Result: 在两个代表性主干上实验，TimeSteer在无需训练的条件下，实现了比现有零训练基线显著更强的时间区间可控性，且整体生成质量保持竞争力。

Conclusion: TimeSteer突破了联合音频-视觉扩散模型的内容生成时间可控性瓶颈，是首个可在不训练情况下实现区间层次语音时序调度的方法，为相关生成任务提供了新的研究与应用方向。

Abstract: Although pretrained joint audio-visual diffusion models offer rich control over \emph{what} to generate, they provide no explicit control over \emph{when} an utterance should occur. To address this, we study \emph{inference-time speech scheduling}, a novel task that places coupled speech and visual articulation within user-specified begin--end intervals without finetuning the backbone model. We uncover two intrinsic properties of the denoising process that enable this task. First, a timing-sensitive text-to-audio cross-attention head exposes each utterance's model-implied source span along the latent timeline. Second, the predicted clean latent already organizes coupled speech and visual articulation, allowing their temporal placement to be edited without regenerating the content. Building on these discoveries, we propose \textbf{TimeSteer}, a training-free framework that localizes each utterance's source span through \textbf{Source Span Localization} and transfers the associated audio-visual latent content from the source interval to the specified target interval through \textbf{Region-Aware Latent Remapping}. We further introduce \textbf{SpeechShift}, the first benchmark for interval-level speech scheduling in joint audio-visual generation. Experiments across two representative backbones show that TimeSteer substantially improves interval controllability over training-free baselines while maintaining competitive overall generation quality.

</details>


### [90] [HiLRP: Toward One Trustworthy Explanation for Vision Transformer: Conservation-Valid Attribution via Attention Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01282)
*Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Pubudu Sanjeewani,Asanka Perera,Selvarajah Thuseethan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的视觉Transformer（ViT）归因方法——HiLRP，能适应多种ViT结构，解决现有方法在不同变体间表现不一致的问题。HiLRP提供了更加可靠且解释性强的归因结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT结构日益多样，不同模型采用了不同的注意力、降采样等模块，导致当前主流归因方法无法在所有类型的ViT模型中可靠工作，急需一种统一、结构无关的归因方法。

Method: 作者分析了现有ViT模型的基本操作模块，将这些操作归纳为四类：线性映射、双线性混合、归一化或门控、重索引，并为每类操作设计了守恒性的归因规则。基于此，提出HiLRP归因框架，能够通过结构组合自适应各种ViT架构。

Result: 在10种架构和14种归因方法的对比中，HiLRP是唯一在所有ViT变体中保持归因守恒的方法，并能准确定位归因失败位置。实验证明HiLRP在EfficientViT上的Pointing分数达0.97，远超其他方法。

Conclusion: HiLRP实现了ViT归因方法的统一和泛化，在结构复杂多样的ViT家族中表现出色，并为模型解释性提供了更为稳健和一致的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) design has become increasingly diverse, with backbones combining convolutional stems, windowed, linear, or multi-axis attention, patch merging, and spatial reduction in various configurations. This diversity poses challenges for existing attribution methods, whose assumptions often do not hold across ViT variants: Grad-CAM requires a terminal spatial feature map, attention rollout assumes global softmax attention, and layer-wise relevance propagation (LRP) requires module-specific rules. To the best of our knowledge, no existing method provides a unified attribution framework across this architectural space. We show that this architectural diversity can be captured by a simpler underlying structure. The attention and resolution-reduction operators in current ViTs can be decomposed into four operation types: linear maps, bilinear mixing, normalization or gating, and reindexing. Each operation admits a relevance rule that satisfies conservation. Based on these rules, HiLRP supports new backbones by construction rather than by architecture-specific derivation, and its attribution maps decompose the prediction rather than relying on heuristic assumptions. We prove conservation and conditional equivariance and verify both to machine precision. Across 14 attribution methods and 10 architectures, we find that no prior method remains reliable across ViT families, while Faithfulness Correlation becomes uninformative for backbones robust to spatial masking. HiLRP alone preserves conservation across windowed, spatial-reduction, multi-axis, and linear-attention models, where naive extensions can produce zero or inflated relevance. It also localizes attribution failures in class activation mapping, achieving 0.97 Pointing compared with 0.55 for competing methods on EfficientViT.

</details>


### [91] [Agentic Multimodal Models for Environmental Hyperspectral Unmixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01289)
*Michał Cholewa,Luca Ciampi,Nicola Messina,Przemysław Głomb,Giuseppe Amato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种基于大规模视觉-语言模型（LVLM）的代理框架，用于对传统高光谱解混流程的结果进行算法无关的精细化优化，从而提高解混效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高光谱解混流程分为模型阶数估计、端元提取和丰度估计多个模块，各阶段误差容易积累导致解混结果存在冗余或歧义，影响最终成分拆分质量。因此，亟需一种能够整合并优化各模块输出的机制。

Method: 作者提出一种LVLM驱动的代理系统，从已有解混结果出发，通过光谱库检索、丰度图可视化等工具收集谱域与空间证据，对端元进行合并或剔除，并重新估算丰度。该框架可用于多种模块化解混流水线，作为独立优化环节存在。

Result: 该方法在HYDICE Urban、Jasper Ridge和Stonewall Playa数据集上应用于不同的解混流水线，结果显示能稳定提升端元数量的准确性，并普遍改进谱签和丰度图的质量，且与端到端解混（如CNN-AE, uDAS, R-CoNMF）相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 工具型LVLM代理能够跨算法整合谱域与空间信息，有效优化高光谱解混分解过程，为现有和未来解混方法提供通用可扩展的算法无关型增强手段。

Abstract: Hyperspectral unmixing is a key task in remote sensing that aims to decompose mixed pixels in hyperspectral images into their constituent material signatures, or endmembers, and their fractional abundances. Conventional modular approaches estimate the scene composition through successive model-order estimation, endmember extraction, and abundance estimation stages, whose errors can lead to redundant or ambiguous candidate components and ultimately affect the recovered decomposition.
